  - `economy`: `silverValue` (10) and `goldValue` (100) of new currencies, `defaultPollTax` (10) and `maxPollTax` (1000), `maxWage` (200), `wages` (usual wages by profession, `profession:amount`), `moodWorkEffect` (0.2), `payConfirmAbove` (1000), `mintToVault` (`true`), `mintRates` (`item=coin:count` or `#tag=coin:count`), `mintSeconds` (15), `itemValues` (`item=value` or `#tag=value`), `defaultItemValue` (5), `laborMarkup` (0.1);
  - `trade`: `exportFactor` (0.6) and `importFactor` (1.5), `exportPerMerchant` (64), `exportExperienceBonus` (1.0), `exportMaxIncomePerDay` (2000), `importMaxPerDay` (256), `importDelayMinutes` (5), `importDenied` (ids and `#tags`), `demandElasticity` (0.5), `minPricePercent` (50) and `maxPricePercent` (300), `priceStockTarget` (64), `spreadPercent` (30), `defaultSalesTaxPercent` (5) and `maxSalesTaxPercent` (25), `maxLotsPerPlayer` (8), `maxMarketEntries` (32), `defaultExchangeFeePercent` (2) and `maxExchangeFeePercent` (10), `minRate` (0.1) and `maxRate` (10), `rateSmoothingPercent` (25), `rateProductionDays` (7), `rateMinSupply` (1000), `caravanBlocksPerMinute` (200), `caravanLossChance` (0.02), `proposalDays` (3), `maxDealsPerKingdom` (16), `maxDealPeriodDays` (30);
  - `player`: `afkTimeoutSeconds`, `combatTagSeconds` (placeholders for later stages);
  - `kingdom`: `allowedDimensions` (`["minecraft:overworld"]`), `minChunksFromOtherKingdom` (4), `initialClaimRadius` (1);
  - `territory`: `baseMaxChunks` (25), `chunksPerMember` (4), `claimBufferChunks` (1; 0 lets borders touch);
  - `protection`: `protectInteractions`, `protectFromExplosions`, `protectFromFire`, `allowFakePlayers` (all `true`);
  - `roads`: `maxCities` (6), `minCityDistance` (160), `maxRoads` (32), `maxRouteLength` (1536), `searchStepsPerTick` (3000), `maxSearchSteps` (400000), `searchChunksPerTick` (6), `maxBridge` (32), `maxFill` (3), `secondsPerBlock` ([1, 3, 5]), `wearPerDay` ([0.004, 0.002, 0.001]), `repairShare` (0.25), `autoRepair` (`true`), `repairBelow` (0.5), `rememberConsent` (`true`), `townPeople` (12), `townBuildings` (6), `cityPeople` (40), `cityBuildings` (20), `wallRadius` (24), `caravanSpeed` ([1.6, 2.5, 3.5]), `speedOnRoads` (`true`), `playerSpeed` and `horseSpeed` ([0.1, 0.2, 0.3]), `crossingDays` (3), `physicalCaravans` (`true`), `bandits` (`true`), `maxCamps` (8), `campMinDistance` (192), `campSpawnChance` (0.2), `campStartBandits` (3), `campMaxBandits` (12), `campGrowthChance` (0.35), `banditStrength` (18), `leaderStrength` (60), `ambushChance` (0.03), `campReach` (320), `ambushByClass` ([0.8, 0.6, 0.45]), `garrisonFactor` (0.35), `patrolFactor` (0.5), `ownLandFactor` (0.6), `protectRadius` (96), `raidParty` (6), `unguardedLoot` (0.75), `merchantDeath` (0.25), `maxPhysicalBandits` (8), `banditWeapons`, `banditArmor`; values by class in the order path, gravel, paved.
  - `npcKingdoms`: `generate` (`true`), `count` (8), `radius` (6000), `minDistance` (1200), `minSpawnDistance` (1000), `triggerChunks` (12), `sizeWeights` ([5, 3, 2]), `landRadius` ([3, 4, 5]), `towns` ([1, 2, 3]), `townDistance` (200), `startingMoney` ([1500, 4000, 10000]), `startingFoodDays` (10), `startingMaterials` ([4, 6, 10]), `soldiers` ([2, 4, 8]), `blocksPerTick` (2000), `chunksPerTick` (4), `maxInhabitedMinutes` (20); values by size in the order small, medium, large.
  - `life`: `workStart` (1000), `eveningStart` (11000), `sleepStart` (14500), `wakeUp` (23200), `spreadTicks` (700), `tavernShare` (50), `tavernMood` (8), `tavernMoodDays` (2), `sickChance` (15), `sickFactor` (2.0), `sickMood` (10), `sickWorkPercent` (50), `infirmaryDays` (1.0), `homeDays` (3.0), `festivalPerCitizen` (20), `festivalFood` (6), `festivalMood` (15), `festivalMoodDays` (3), `festivalEveryDays` (7), `daysPerYear` (96), `fairEveryDays` (7), `fairLevel` (`city`), `fairSeats` (2), `fairDiscount` (100), `fairVisitorItems` (64), `roomPrice` (30), `rumorPrice` (15), `rumorsMin` (2), `rumorsMax` (4); times in ticks of the day, money in copper coins of the kingdom.
  - `npcKingdoms.rulers`: `turnEveryDays` (1), `decisionsPerTurn` (3), `decreesPerDay` (2), `maxChunks` ([96, 160, 256]), `chunksPerClaim` (4), `freeBeds` (1), `storageFullPercent` (85), `roles` (storehouse, market, well, workshop, barracks, watchtower, tavern, mage tower by level), `townPopulation` (40), `maxTowns` (4), `reserveDays` (5), `soldiersPermille` (100), `slackPermille` (100), `roadDistance` (1200), `festivalMood` (35).
  - `war`: `preparationMinutes` (20), `maxWarDays` (7), `pactDays` (10), `truceDays` (10), `proposalMinutes` (20), `stepSeconds` (60), `battleChance` (0.35), `raidChance` (0.25), `exhaustionPerDay` (8), `imposeLead` (40), `tributePermille` (200), `tributeDays` (5), `vassalPermille` (100), `indemnityPermille` (300), `absenceMinutes` (5), `fightGraceSeconds` (60), `protectPeacefulPlayers` (`true`), `npcWarDays` (10), `npcRecentWarDays` (5), `npcWarsOnPlayers` (`true`), `npcPlayerWarDays` (20);
  - `maps`: `snapshotMaxChunks` (32768, chunks of land a client map gets at once) and `snapshotRadiusChunks` (64, the square around the player when there is more), `blueMap` and `dynmap` (`true`), `webMapCaravans` (`true`), `webMapFillOpacity` (0.3), `webMapBorderWidth` (2), `webMapLanguage` (`en_us`), `webMapUpdateSeconds` (5).
- `config/kingdomworld-common.toml`: `disabledCompatModules`, mod ids whose compat modules stay off.
- `config/kingdomworld-client.toml`: `requestKingdomListOnJoin`, `showBorderMessages` (messages when crossing borders), `showPartyList` (the list of the party in the corner); `maps`: `journeyMap` and `xaeroMaps` (`true`), `mapCaravans` (`true`), `mapFillOpacity` (0.25), `mapBorderWidth` (2.0).

### Data and data safety

Kingdom data is stored in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_kingdoms.dat`, format 9 (roles and levels of the buildings of the register, their wood and stone, the blocks found missing and Repairs itself; the kinds of constructions (a new building, an upgrade, a repair, a demolition) with their building, the blocks they skip and what a demolition took down; the blueprints of kingdoms; constructions with their template, place, turn, priority, steps done and paid, materials at the site and orders, the register of buildings with integrity; the market of every kingdom with its lists, lots and orders from abroad, deals, caravans on the way and goods waiting for players; reserves of other currencies in treasuries, exchange rates, payments of deals in escrow and coins in hands; the treasury of every kingdom with its currency, books, vault of coins, journal, tax, wages and debts, the accounts of players, old currencies, the pay of every citizen on the last payday; kingdoms with land, members, rights, applications and invitations, citizens with their beds, workplaces and the state of their work, the chronicle, work posts with their settings, mode, workstations and yields, the record of production, orders, the contents of every storehouse and the gear of citizens). Data of formats 1-8 is upgraded automatically; the upgrade from format 2 gives the steward the citizens right, the upgrade from format 3 adds empty posts, records and gear, the upgrade from format 4 gives the steward the orders right, posts work on their own and there are no orders yet; the upgrade from format 5 gives the treasurer the Treasury right and kingdoms an empty treasury with the default currency whose first payday only starts the count, and nobody has an account yet; the upgrade from format 6 gives the treasurer and the steward the Market right and the treasurer and the chancellor the right of deals with kingdoms, there are no markets, deals or reserves yet and every currency starts at the rate of one; the upgrade from format 7 gives the steward the Construction right, there are no constructions or buildings yet; the upgrade from format 8 gives every building of the register the role its type names (if there is one) and level 1, constructions build new buildings, no kingdom has own blueprints, and no right changes; nothing else changes. Reservations of ingredients are not saved: after a restart nothing is set aside.

The guild is stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_guild.dat`, format 1: members with ranks and points, the adventurers among the citizens, branches (commission, budget), contracts with their escrow and squads (also those away, with the time they come back). Loading the guild never throws either: data of a newer version, a damaged version or an unreadable entry keep the guild read-only (`/kingdomworld admin guild unlock`, with a backup). The marshal gets the right "Adventurers' guild" once: the kingdom data remembers the upgrades done in the list `upgrades`, without a new format number.

The army is stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_army.dat`, format 1: soldiers (experience, kills, battles), squads with their commander and order, bands of mercenaries on offer and hired with the day they are paid until, barracks and the day of the last offers. The gear of soldiers is kept as the gear of citizens. Loading the army never throws: data of a newer format, a damaged version, an unreadable entry or a squad without its soldiers or band keep the army read-only (`/kingdomworld admin army unlock` with a backup). The marshal gets the "Army" right once (the upgrade `army_rights` in the list `upgrades`; the format of the kingdoms does not change). Network protocol: version 10.

Companions and parties are stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_companions.dat`, format 1: the records of companions with their packs, loyalty, hunger, wages and orders, the offers of the markets of the branches, closed markets, parties with their shares and contracts, invitations. Anything not understood (an item of a removed mod in a pack included) keeps the companion data read-only: nothing is lost and the file is not overwritten, and `/kingdomworld admin companions unlock` keeps what could be read after backing up the original.

The law is stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_law.dat`, format 1: the wanted with their crimes, the decisions of the court and the contracts for heads, confiscated goods with the goods themselves, reputations and the trade that counts toward them, passes into cities, the laws of the courts and who pays entry by themselves; debts live in the treasuries. Anything not understood (an item of a mod that is gone among confiscated goods too) keeps the law read-only: nothing is lost and the file is not overwritten, `/kingdomworld admin law unlock` keeps what was read after a copy of the original. The chancellor of an old world gets the "Court" right once (upgrade `law_rights`, the format number of the kingdoms stays). Network protocol: version 15. Reports on the way live in memory only: a restart within those 10-30 seconds forgets them.

Magic is stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_magic.dat`, format 1: the energy of the kingdoms with the journal, research, the spells switched on and what they were paid for, the chunks of the ward, the blocks of magic on kingdom land (foci, accumulators, generators, gates) and the spells players learned. The energy left in started crystals travels with the crystals. Data that cannot be read or is newer is loaded read-only (changes are refused, the file is not overwritten) until `/kingdomworld admin magic unlock`. The court mage of older kingdoms gets the right `manage_magic` once on load (the upgrade `magic_rights`).

Roads are stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_roads.dat`, format 1: cities, roads (the way block by block, sections with who builds them, paid and placed blocks, the items waiting to be placed, condition and repairs), requests to cross land and remembered consents, city walls (the ring on the lay of the land, gates, sections with what is paid and placed, the number in the register of buildings), camps (robbers, the leader, the loot, who knows of it, the blocks of the camp and its chest) and the ways of caravans with the pieces already passed. Loading never throws: data of a newer format, a damaged version or an unreadable entry keep the roads read-only (`/kingdomworld admin roads unlock` with a backup). Robbers, merchants of caravans and pack llamas are not saved with chunks (llamas of an earlier run go when loaded): camps and caravans bring them back near players. The format of the kingdom data did not change. Network protocol: version 15.

Kingdoms of the world are stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_npc.dat`, format 2 (format 1 is read as it is, its rulers starting with an empty memory): the memories of rulers (decrees, the chronicle, needs, decisions waiting, relations to neighbours, dealings with players), the plan (where the planner stopped, whether it is made), the places (position, size, seed, places of towns, state, kingdom, stage and step of the appearance, height of the square, why a place was left alone; the towns that stay) and the records of the kingdoms of the world (the mark of a kingdom of the world, names in both alphabets, the ruler with a leaning and traits, towns, the day it appeared and the day of the ruler's last turn). Loading never throws: data of a newer format, a damaged version or an unreadable entry keep them read-only (`/kingdomworld admin npc unlock` with a backup). The category "Founding" (`founding`) is added at the end of the journal of treasuries. The format of the kingdom data did not change. Network protocol: version 19.

The life of cities is stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_life.dat`, format 1: the ill with how far they got, the last evening of every citizen in a tavern, the festivals of kingdoms with the chronicle of festivals and fairs, the days of the fairs of cities and the lots of goods brought to them, rooms of players in taverns with their point of return before, and the camps marked on atlases. Loading never throws: data of a newer format, a damaged version or an unreadable entry keep it read-only (`/kingdomworld admin life unlock` with a backup). The record of a citizen and the format of the kingdom data did not change. Network protocol: version 18.

Diplomacy is stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_diplomacy.dat`, format 1: relations with their reasons, treaties, offers, wars with their sides, allies, calls, score, exhaustion and outcome, tributes, claims of land, cities and thrones for the next stage, the chronicle of wars and treaties, when the players of each kingdom were last online and fought, and the times of the wars of rulers of the world. Loading never throws: data of a newer format, a damaged version or an unreadable entry keep diplomacy read-only (`/kingdomworld admin war unlock` with a backup). The "Diplomacy" right goes to the chancellor and "Declare war" stays with the king; old worlds get the first once (upgrade `diplomacy_rights`, the format number of the kingdoms stays). Ledger line "War". Network protocol: version 20.

The nobility is stored apart, in `<world>/data/kingdomworld_nobility.dat`, format 1: houses, titles, courts, demands, plots and spies. Loading never throws: data it cannot understand keeps the nobility read-only (`/kingdomworld admin nobility unlock` with a backup). Ledger line "Nobility". Network protocol: version 22.

If the data cannot be fully read (a format newer than this version understands, a damaged format version, a failed migration, an unreadable kingdom, citizen, storehouse, treasury or account, or an unreadable file), loading does not throw: the data becomes read-only. What could be read stays available and keeps protecting land, but every change is refused with an ERROR in the log, the citizen simulation stops, storehouses stay closed, and the file is never overwritten. Fix the file or run `/kingdomworld admin data unlock` to leave read-only mode.

### For map mod integrations

The map mods are wired in the `compat` package (see "Map mods"). Other mods and later stages can use:

- server: `KingdomWorldApi.claims(server, dimension)` returns every claimed chunk of a dimension with its owner (chunk keys as `ChunkPos.toLong()`), `KingdomWorldApi.addTerritoryListener(...)` reports every change of owner;
- client: `ClientTerritoryCache` holds the owners of the chunks it asked for and facts about kingdoms (color, capital, ruler) and accepts listeners as well; `ClientMapSync.enable(...)` keeps the land of the whole dimension known (snapshot and deltas), `ClientMapModel` gives the outlines of kingdoms and reports changes;
- core: `ChunkOutlines` outlines land made of chunks (areas with holes, enclaves), `TerritoryShapes` redoes that only for kingdoms that changed;
- visibility: `KingdomWorldApi.setMapVisibility(...)` decides which kingdoms a player sees on maps (the atlas, map mods; web maps ask without a player);
- war layers: `MapZones.register(...)` adds contested land and battle zones to the web maps, `ClientMapZones` to the client maps.
- roles of buildings: `KingdomWorldApi.registerBuildingRole(...)` adds a role (the barracks of the army, the tower of the mages, roles of mods), `KingdomWorldApi.workingBuildings(...)` gives the working buildings of a kingdom with a role and its numbers, `towerSight(...)` and `fortification(...)` the sight from towers and the strength of walls.

### Code layout

Package `io.github.sekerator.kingdomworld`:

- `core`: plain Java domain logic without Minecraft (`core.trade`: the market, prices and the spread, lots, rates and exchange, deals, caravans and escrow; kingdoms, territory and claim rules, membership and rights, the access policy, request validation; `core.production` holds recipes as the planner sees them, orders, chains and reservations; `core.economy` currencies, coins, the treasury and its journal, accounts, wages, taxes, plural forms and the engine of item values). Fast to unit test.
- `common`: everything that runs on both sides (`common.trade`: the stall and the merchant, what the market and the stall do, trade abroad, exchange at banks, deals and caravans over time): registries, persistence (`SavedData` with a format version, migrations and read-only mode), networking, commands, config, the server API, protection and game tests; `common.citizen` (bodies, simulation, places, gear) with `common.citizen.work` (professions, work posts, measured and abstract gathering, zone estimates, carriers) and `common.storage` (storehouses and `KingdomStorage`), `common.production` (the game's recipes, workstations, workshops, orders), `common.economy` (the coin, the bank, the mint and the minter, the payday, item values of mods, what players do with the treasury and the bank). `common.mixin` holds one mixin for fire, because Forge has no event for fire spreading.
- `core.law` (crimes and the wanted, the decision whether a deed is a crime, rules and fines, reputation, the plan of a fine, confiscated goods, the laws of courts, the entry into cities and the `CityGates` interface) and `common.law` (witnesses and reports, guards, thefts from containers, punishments, ransom, the ban of slavery, the entry into cities, data, windows and commands): the law.
- `core.companion` (companions, the market, strength and price, loyalty, wages, parties; the split of rewards in `core.guild.RewardSplit`) and `common.companion` (bodies, fighting, the pack and gear, food, data, commands, windows and packets).
- `core.combat` (strength of fighters, battles of two sides, the risk of caravans), `core.army` (soldiers, squads, orders, mercenaries, the choice of gear, the "Army" right) and `common.army` (the professions of soldiers and their goals, gear of any mod, battles far from players, guards of caravans, the barracks, the baton, the tab and the commands): the army.
- `core.road` (cities and their level, roads and sections, the layout of blocks, the search for a way over the land, the network of roads and the way of a caravan, ambushes, camps and their growth, consent to cross land, walls and their ring) and `common.road` (the town hall, the robber and the merchant of caravans, planning and building roads, walls and coming into a city, speed, caravans on roads, camps in the world, the tab, packets, commands): roads and robbers.
- `core.npc` (the plan of places from the seed, the size and make-up of a kingdom, names in both alphabets, the wealth at the start, the ruler with a character, the stages of an appearance) and `common.npc` (judging places by the noise of the generator, appearing over ticks with chunk tickets, levelling the ground, commands, data, the daily turn of rulers): kingdoms of the world.
- `client`: client-only code (founding, throne and map screens, the key binding, the territory cache). Wired in on the client only; a dedicated server never loads these classes.
- `compat`: soft integrations with other mods. A module is activated only when its mod is installed. `compat.maps` (shared by the web maps: the model, texts in the server's language, icons), `compat.bluemap`, `compat.dynmap`, and the client modules `compat.journeymap` and `compat.xaero`, which are created on the client only. The APIs of map mods are needed at compile time only.

Textures and models of the market stall, the crown, the throne, the atlas, the storehouse, the infirmary, the work post, the citizens, the coins, the bank and the mint are placeholders.
